nāṭakattamiḻ (Dramatic Literature)
nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
 pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam	(tol. akat. 56)
kūttar viṟaliyar. . . . . vāyilkaḷ eṉpa    (tol. kaṟpu. 52)
eṉpavaṟṟāl, tolkāppiyar kālattu nāṭakamuṇmi yaṟiyap paṭum.
nāṭakam eṉpatu naṭi eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyāyp piṟanta toḻiṟpeyar. naṭi + akam = nāṭakam. (mutaṉili tirintu īṟupeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar). naṭi + am = naṭam - naṭṭam. naṭṭam-niruttam (va). o.nō: vaṭṭam - viruttam (va.). (vaḷ + tam = vaṭṭam). nāṭakam kati taḻuvivarum kūttu. naṭam naṭṭam eṉpaṉa taṉikkūttum pāṭṭiṟkēṟṟa apinayamuḷḷatum. naṭṭam payiṟṟu pavaṉ naṭṭuvaṉ. naṭam payilpavaḷ kaṇiki. nāṭakamāṭupavaḷ nāṭakak kaṇiki. tāḷattik kaṇittāṭuvatāl kaṇiki
yeṉṟu peyar. kaṇikiyi ikkālattil tāciyeṉpar. tāci = aṭiyāḷ, tēvaṭiyāḷ. taṉikkūttiṟkut tāṇṭavam eṉṉum peyar. tāṇṭiyāṭuvatu tāṇṭavam. tāṇṭutal - kutittal.
naṭi eṉpatu naṭa eṉṉum colliṉ tiripu. mutaṉmutal naṭittatu oruvaṉippōl naṭantu kāṭṭiyatē. naṭa eṉṉum collē am īṟupeṟṟu naṭam eṉṟāṉatu eṉiṉum poruntum. naṭakkiṟa iṭam eṉpati naṭamāṭukiṟa iṭam eṉṟu collum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
naṭattal eṉpatu, kālāl naṭattali maṭṭumaṉṟi oḻukum vakiyiyuṅ kuṟikkum. naṭakki, naṉṉaṭakki mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
nāṭakattit taṉikkūttu, pāṭṭoṭu kūṭiyatu, kati taḻuviyatu eṉa mūṉṟāy vakukkalām.
nāṭakattiṟkuk kūttu eṉṟum peyar. kūttu eṉpatu mutalāvatu āṭṭatti maṭṭum uṇartti, piṉpu kati taḻuviya nāṭakattiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. kutittāṭuvatu kūttu. kūttāṭu kiṟāṉ, āṉanta (uvaki)k kūttāṭiṉāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
nāṭakatti muṉṉōr (1) vacikkūttu, pukaḻkkūttu, 
(2) vēttiyaṟkūttu, potuviyaṟkūttu, (3) varikkūttu, variccāntik kūttu, (4) cāntikkūttu, viṉōta (iṉpa)k kūttu, 
(5) āriyak kūttu, tamiḻakkūttu, (6) iyalpukūttu, tēcikakkūttu, (7) akakkūttu, puṟakkūttu eṉap palavakiyil ivviraṇṭāka vakuttaṉar.
iṉi, ulakiyaṟkūttu, tēviyaṟkūttu eṉa vakukkavum iṭamuṇṭu. tēviyalāvaṉa araṅkēṟṟukātiyiṟ kūṟappaṭum 
11 āṭalkaḷ pōlvaṉa. cāntikkūttiṉ vaki :
(1)	cokkam - taṉinaṭam (cuttaniruttam).
(2)	mey - tēci, vaṭuku, ciṅkaḷam.
(3)	avinayam - katitaḻuvātu pāṭṭiṉ poruḷukkēṟpa vallapañceyvatu.
(4)	nāṭakam - kati taḻuvivaruvatu.
avinayam (apinayam) eṉṉuñcol ōr irupiṟappi.
vinōtakkūttiṉ vaki
(1)	kuravi
(2)	kalinaṭam - kaḻāykkūttu
(3)	kuṭakkūttu
(4)	karaṇam
(5)	nōkku - māyam, kaṇkaṭṭu mutaliyaṉa.
(6)	tōṟpāvi
(7)	nakittiṟaccuvi.
cilar nakittiṟaccuvikkup patilāka veṟiyāṭṭik kūṟuvar.
varikkūttu kaṇkūṭuvari, kāṇvari, uḷvari, puṟavari, kiḷarvari, tērccivari, kāṭcivari, eṭuttukkōḷvari eṉa eṭṭuvakippaṭum.
avinayam vekuṇṭōṉ, aiyamuṟṟōṉ, cōmpiṉōṉ, kaḷittōṉ, uvantōṉ, aḻukkāṟuṭiyōṉ, iṉpamuṟṟōṉ, teyvamuṟṟōṉ, ñaññiyuṟṟōṉ, uṭaṉpaṭṭōṉ, uṟaṅkiṉōṉ, tuyiluṇarntōṉ, cettōṉ, maḻipeyyap paṭṭōṉ, paṉittalippaṭṭōṉ, veyiṟṟalippaṭṭōṉ, nāṇamuṟṟōṉ, varuttamuṟṟōṉ, kaṇṇōvuṟṟōṉ, talinōvuṟṟōṉ, aḻaṟṟiṟappaṭṭōṉ, cītamuṟṟōṉ, veppamuṟṟōṉ, nañcuṇṭōṉ eṉa 24 vakikaḷiyuṭiyatu. ivaṟṟi iṉṉum maliyāḷa nāṭṭuk katakaḷiyiṟ kāṇalām.
avinayattiṟkuriya ki iṇiyāviṉikki (piṇṭi, oṟṟikki), iṇikki (iraṭṭikki, piṇiyal) eṉa iruvaki. ivaṟṟōṭu āṇki, peṇki, alikki, potukki eṉa nāṉkik kūṭṭuvārumuḷar,
iṇiyāviṉikki patāki mutal valampuriyīṟāka 
33 vakippaṭum. iṇikki añcali mutal varuttamāṉam īṟāka 15 vakippaṭum.
iṉi, avinayakki eḻiṟki, toḻiṟki, poruṭki eṉa mūṉṟākavum vakukkappaṭum.
nāṭakamāṭum cāli araṅku eṉappaṭum. ic col ar eṉṉum olikkuṟippiṉiṉṟum piṟantu, aṟi eṉṉum poruḷiyuṭiyatu. aṟiyi araṅku eṉpatu iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
araṅkiṟ kaṭṭum tiri eḻiṉi yeṉappaṭum. mēl eḻuntu celvatāl eḻiṉi yeṉappaṭṭatu. atu orumuka veḻiṉi (orupuṟattiṉiṉṟu iḻuppatu), porumuka veḻiṉi (iru puṟattiṉiṉṟum iḻukkum), iraṭṭittiri. karantuvaral eḻiṉi (mēṉiṉṟu kīḻiṟaṅki varuvatu) eṉa mūvakippaṭum. vaṭanūl kaḷil orēyoruvakit tirimaṭṭum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatākac collappaṭukiṟatu.
nāṭakampaṟṟiya piṟa vilakkaṇaṅkaḷi yellām, aṭiyārkkunallār araṅkēṟṟu kātik kuritta vuri yiṉiṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
iciyilum nāṭakattilum itupōtu vaḻaṅkum  vaṭacol mikutiyik kaṇṭu, ivi āriyakkalikaḷō eṉṟu aiyuṟaṟka. ivaṟṟi āriyakkalikaḷākak kāṭṭavēṇṭiyē, teṉcoṟ kaṭkup patilāka vaṭacoṟkaḷip pukuttiviṭṭaṉar āriyar eṉka. kural eṉpatikkūṭac cārīram eṉṟu coṉṉāl piṟavaṟṟip paṟṟi eṉ colvatu?
icittamiḻum nāṭakattamiḻum maṟintupōṉamikku āriyamum camaṇamum perituṅ kāraṇamākum.
tolkāppiyattiṉiṉṟu aṟiyum pākkaḷum nūlkaḷum
aṟuvakippākkaḷ (Poetic Metres)
veṇpā, āciriyappā, kalippā, vañcippā, maruṭpā, paripāṭal eṉpaṉa.
irupāṉ vaṇṇaṅkaḷ (Poetic Rhythms)
pāa vaṇṇam, tāa vaṇṇam, vallici vaṇṇam, mellici vaṇṇam,  iyipu vaṇṇam, aḷapeṭi vaṇṇam, neṭuñcīr vaṇṇam, kuṟuñcīr vaṇṇam, cittira (ōviya) vaṇṇam, nalipu vaṇṇam, akappāṭṭu vaṇṇam, puṟappāṭṭu vaṇṇam, oḻuku vaṇṇam, orūu vaṇṇam, eṇṇu vaṇṇam, akippu vaṇṇam, tūṅkal vaṇṇam, ēntal vaṇṇam, uruṭṭu vaṇṇam, muṭuku vaṇṇam eṉpaṉa.
poruḷīṭukaḷum nūlvakikaḷum 
(Different Themes and Kinds of Poetry)
kāmap poruḷīṭukaḷ (Amatory Themes)
kikkiḷi, aintiṇi, peruntiṇi, maṭal mutaliyaṉa. 
varaṇaṉip poruḷīṭukaḷ (Descriptive Themes)
kuṟiñci, mulli (Pastoral), marutam, neytal, pāli eṉpaṉa.
pōrp poruḷīṭukaḷ (Martial Themes)
veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi, vāki mutaliyaṉa.
karutaṟ poruḷīṭukaḷ (Reflective Themes)
peruṅkāñci, mutukañci, maṟakkāñci, vañciṉak kāñci, makaṭpāṟkāñci, kiyaṟunili (Elegy) mutaliyaṉa.
pāṭāṇ poruḷīṭukaḷ (Euology and Doxology)
ulā, koṟṟavaḷḷi, iyaṉmoḻi vāḻttu, nālvaki yāṟṟuppaṭi, kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu mutaliyaṉa.
vāyuṟi vāḻttu, ceviyaṟivuṟūu (Didactic.)
puṟanili vāḻttu (Benedictory.)
ceyyuḷiṉ eḻunilam (Bases of Poetical Composition)
pāṭṭu (Poem or Lyric), uri (Prose), nūl (Grammar and 
Science), vāymoḻi (Scripture), pici (Riddle), aṅkatam (Satire and 
Imprecation), mutucol (Proverb) eṉpaṉa.
ivaṟṟuḷ, uri pāṭṭiṭi vitta kuṟippu (Prose interposed in poetry), pāviṉṟeḻunta kiḷavi (Commentary), poruḷ marapillāp poymmoḻi (Fable), poruḷoṭu puṇarnta nakimoḻi (Wits and Humour) eṉa nālvakippaṭum.
nūlvaki (Different Kinds of Poems)
paṇṇatti - pāṭṭum uriyum kalanta oruvaki nūl.
eṇ vaṉappu
ammi (Sonnet), aḻaku (Poem with proper diction), toṉmi (Legend in mixed prose & verse), tōl (Epic), viruntu (Novel 
Composition), iyipu (Epic whose sections end in any of the nasals or liquids), pulaṉ (Dramatic), iḻipu (Lyrical) eṉpaṉa.
maṟinūl (Scripture)
ovvoru nāṭṭilum mutalāvatu tōṉṟakkūṭiya ciṟanta nūl maṟinūlākum. ataṉāl ataṉ peyar aṟivu allatu puttakam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatāyiruntu, piṟkālattil piṟa nūlkaḷukkum kalikaḷukkum potup peyarāvatuṇṭu.
āṅkilattil kiṟittava maṟinūlukku Bible eṉṟu peyar. itu puttakam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum biblos eṉṉum kirēkkac colliṉiṉṟum piṟantatu.
Bible eṉṉum col iṉṟum puttakam eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuvati, bibliography, bibliomania mutaliya coṟkaḷā laṟiyalām. Scripture eṉpatum mutalāvatu eḻuttu (eḻutappaṭṭa nūl) eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. Script - eḻuttu.
vaṭamoḻiyil vētamē mutalāvatu nūl. vētam eṉṉum peyar, vit (aṟi) eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu piṟantu, aṟivu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatoru col. piṟkālattil āyurvētam, kāntaruva vētam, taṉurvētam eṉṟu piṟa kalikaṭkum vētam eṉṉum col potuppeyarāyiṟṟu.
iṅṅaṉamē tamiḻilum mutalāvateḻunta nūl maṟi nūlākum. maṟi eṉṉum col maṟivāṉa (Esoteric) poruḷuḷḷatu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu. piṉpu atu piṟa kalikaṭkum potup peyarāṉati, narampiṉ maṟi eṉṟum, kāmap puṇarcciyum.... maṟiyeṉa moḻital maṟiyōr āṟē17 eṉṟum, muṟiyē, icinūliyum ilakkaṇa nūliyum kuṟikka ac collit tolkāppiyar vaḻaṅkiyatiṉiṉ ṟaṟiyalām.
tamiḻarkkut taṉimaṟiyirunta teṉpatu, tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷiyalil,
pāṭṭuri nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē 	(75)
maṟimoḻi kiḷanta mantirat tāṉa 	(158)
niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta
 maṟimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa 	(171)
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatā laṟiyappaṭum.
 maṟinū lilakkaṇam tolkāppiyar kūṟiyapaṭiyē tiruvaḷḷuvarum  kūṟukiṉṟār.
niṟimoḻi māntar perumi nilattu
 maṟimoḻi kāṭṭi viṭum 	(kuṟaḷ. 28)
nakkīrar pāṭiya muraṇil potiyil eṉṉum ceyyuḷi mantiramaṉṟeṉṟum aṅkatap pāṭṭeṉṟum pērāciriyar kūṟiyiruppatālum, `nīttār perumi eṉṉum atikārattil muṉivar cāvippit taṉiyāy,
kuṇameṉṉuṅ kuṉṟēṟi niṉṟār vekuḷi
 kaṇamēyuṅ kātta laritu 	(kuṟaḷ. 29)
17	tol cey. 178.
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatālum, niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta maṟimoḻi eṉpatu, muṉivaruṭiya vāḻvippuc cāvippāṟṟalik kuṟiyātu, makkaḷ kikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya vitiyāka, allatu kaṭavuḷiṉ kaṭṭaḷiyāka, muṉivar kūṟiya maṟiporuḷuḷḷa kūṟṟukaḷiyē kuṟippatākum.
pakkuvāṉmākkaḷukkaṉṟip piṟarkkaṟivikkappaṭāmal maṟikkappaṭṭataṉāl maṟimoḻi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
maṟi, maṟinūl eṉpaṉa tamiḻar vētattiyum, maṟimoḻi eṉpatu av vētak kūṟṟiyum, vāymoḻi, mantiram eṉpaṉa av vētac ceyyuḷkaḷiyum kuṟippaṉavākum.
nammāḻvār vāymoḻiyeṉṟa colliyum, tirumūlar mantiram eṉṉum colliyum terintukoṇṭaṉar.
vāymoḻi eṉpatu vāykkum allatu niṟivēṟum colleṉṟum, mantiram eṉpatu maṉattiṉ tirampaṟṟiya colleṉṟum poruḷpaṭum.
maṉam, tiram eṉpaṉa tamiḻccoṟkaḷē. muṉṉutal - niṉittal. muṉ + am = muṉṉam - muṉam.
A.S. munan, to thinks. Ger. meinen, to think. muṉam - maṉam 
(muṉ - maṉ) o.nō: muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku.
muṟumuṟu - marmar (murmur), E.
kd« - kd° (Skt.), mens (L.), menos (Gk.), mind (E.).
tiram = uṟuti. tiram - tiṟam. ra - ṟa. ō.nō: oḷir - oḷiṟu; karu(ppu) - kaṟu(ppu). tiram eṉpatiyē sakaramey mutaṟ cērttu stiram eṉṟaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāriyar. o.nō. nākam - snake (E).
muṉ - maṉ. maṉ + tiram = mantiram.
o.nō: tēṉ + taṭṭu = tēntaṭṭu.
vāymoḻi mantiram eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ oruporuṭ coṟkaḷākat tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭiṉum, vaḻakkup poruḷil avaṟṟukku vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. atu piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum.
ellā veḻuttum veḷippaṭak kiḷantu
 colliya paḷḷi eḻutaru vaḷiyiṟ
 piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi uṟaḻcci vārat
 takatteḻu vaḷiyici ariltapa nāṭi
 aḷapiṟ kōṭal antaṇar maṟittē
 aḵtivaṇ nuvalā teḻuntupuṟat ticikkum
 meyteri vaḷiyici aḷavunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē   (tol. piṟappu.20)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyap piṟappiyaliṟ kūṟiyatu tamiḻa maṟiyiyē yaṉṟi āriya maṟiyiyaṉṟu.
mutalāvatu, antaṇar eṉṉum col muṉivarik kuṟitta teṉṟum, tolkāppiyam ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvatu tamiḻ eḻuttu kaṭkēyeṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
iraṇṭāvatu, aḵtivaṇ nuvalātu.... aḷavunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē eṉpatāl, nuvalattakka eḻuttoliyiyē nuvalāta tākak kūṟukiṉṟār tolkāppiyar eṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum. tolkāppiyattil nuvalattakka eḻuttukaḷ tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷē. ākiyāl, oppīṭṭu (Comparative) muṟiyālum āriya veḻuttup piṟappuk kuṟikkappaṭavilli.
tolkāppiyar kālattil āriya moḻiyil ilakkaṇa miruntatilli yeṉṟu muṉṉamē kūṟappaṭṭatu.
akatteḻu vaḷiyici.... nuvalātu,
puṟatticikkum meyteri vaḷiyici....
nuvaṉṟiciṉē eṉṟu kūṟuvatāl, vāyiṉiṉṟum veḷippaṭṭolikkiṉṟa vaṭivuteriyum eḻuttoliyik kūṟukiṉṟāreṉṟum, aṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭātu uḷḷēyē olikkiṉṟa eḻuttoliyik kūṟavilliyeṉṟum terikiṉṟatu.
vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷellām puṟatticittu mey (vaṭivu) terikiṉṟa volikaḷātaliṉ, avaṟṟit tol kāppiyar kuṟikkavilli yeṉpatu tēṟṟam.
piṉ vēṟu poruḷeṉ eṉiṉ, kūṟukiṉṟēṉ. tamiḻ nāṭṭil, muṉivarum yōkiyaru miruntamiyum avar yōku payiṉṟamiyum, maṟuvil ceyti mūvakik kālamum neṟiyi ṉāṟṟiya aṟivaṉ tēyamum, nāliru vaḻakkiṟ ṟāpatap pakka mum, antaṇar, niṟimoḻi māntar eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟuvatālaṟiyalām.
yōkam tamiḻil tapam allatu tavam eṉappaṭum. taputtal (piṟappik) keṭuttal. tapu + am = tapam - tavam. o.nō: tapal- taval.  tiruvaḷḷuvar tavam eṉṉuñ colli yōkam eṉṉum poruḷilēyē vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṟār. tolkāppiyarum atē poruḷil ataṉ maṟuvaṭivāṉa tapam eṉpati vaḻaṅkiyiruk kiṟār. tapam ceyvār tapattōr allatu tāpatar eṉappaṭuvar.
tapattil, eṇṇam iṟivaṉmēlēyē yiruppatāl, viṉikkuk kāraṇamāka avāvum, ataṉ kāriyamākiya viṉiyum, ataṉ payaṉāṉa piṟappum tapukiṉṟaṉa. ituvē tapam. tuṟavoḻukkattiṉ mukkiya nili tavamē. tavamillāviṭiṉ tuṟattalaritu. itaṉālēyē, tuṟavaṟaviyalil, tavatti muṉṉum tuṟavu avāvaṟuttalkaḷip piṉṉum vittuk kūṟiṉār tiruvaḷḷuvar. illaṟattiṉiṉṟum tuṟavaṟatti vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvatum tavaniliyē. 
tapam eṉṉuñ col mutalāvatu tuṟavunili muḻuvatiyuṅ kuṟittup piṉpu atil mukkiyap pakutiyāṉa yōkuniliyi maṭṭuṅ kuṟittatu. tuṟavaṟattippaṟṟiya pakuti muḻumikkum tuṟavaṟaviyal eṉṟu peyariṭṭu, atil tuṟavu eṉa oru taṉiyatikāramum amittirukkiṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. iṅṅaṉamē tavam eṉṉum peyarum eṉka.
tapam - tapas (va). o.nō: maṉam-maṉas (va.).
tavattiṟkuriya eṇkūṟukaḷ iyamam, niyamam, ācaṉam, vaḷinili, tokinili, poṟinili, niṉital, camāti eṉak kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
ivaṟṟuḷ mutal mūṉṟum kaṭiyoṉṟum vaṭacoṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟukkup patilākak kaṭivu, nōṉpu, irukki, kalattal eṉpavaṟṟi muṟiyē vittukkoḷḷalām. piṟkālattār eṇṇuṟuppukaḷiyum vaṭacoṟkaḷālēyē kūṟuvar. poruṭ ṭokikaḷi mutaliliruntu kavaṉittukkoṇṭē vantāl, tolkāppiyar kālattil iraṇṭoru vaṭacoṟkaḷē tōṉṟip piṉpu varavara mikkut taṟkālattil, poruṭ pākupāṭukaḷellām āriyamō eṉṟu mayaṅkumpaṭiyā yiruppatu tōṉṟum.
tavattiṟkuriya eṇṇuṟuppukaḷil vaḷinili mika mukkiyamāṉatu. itip pirāṇāyāmam eṉpar vaṭanūlār.
uṇmūccu, veḷimūccu eṉṉum iruvaki mūccukaḷil muṉṉatu oru maṭaṅkum piṉṉatu oṉṟari maṭaṅkumākum. iv viraṇṭiyum camamākkiviṭṭāl vāḻnāḷ nīṭikkum eṉpatu muṉṉōr karuttu. aṅṅaṉam camamākki iruvaki mūccukaḷiyum nikaḻttumpōtē, kaṭavuḷ peyarāṉa civa eṉṉum īreḻuttu kaḷiyum mantiramāka volittukkoḷḷumpaṭiyāṉa muṟiyi yum avarkaḷ vakuttārkaḷ. iyalpākak kuṟukiyum nīṇṭumiruk kiṉṟa uṇmūccu veḷimūccukaḷ, kuṟilum neṭilumākavuḷḷa civā eṉṉum viḷippeyar pōlva veṉṟum. avaṟṟic civa eṉṉum viḷivaṭivi yokkumāṟu camappaṭuttikkoḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, av viruvaki mūccukaḷiyum civa eṉṉum īreḻuttolikaḷākak karutuvatuṭaṉ, av volikaḷākavē am mūccukaḷip puṟattārkkup pulaṉākātapaṭi mellitāy uyirkka vēṇṭu meṉṟum, ataṉāl uṭalukku iḷamiyum āṉmāviṟku vīṭupēṟum kiṭṭum eṉṟum muṉṉōr koṇṭaṉar. ivaṟṟi,
kūṭa meṭuttuk kuṭipukka maṅkiya
 rōṭuvar mīḷuvar paṉṉiraṇ ṭaṅkula
 nīṭuva reṇviraṟ kaṇṭippar nālviral
 kūṭik koḷiṟkōla vañceḻut tāmē	(tiruman. 576)
vaḷiyiṉi vāṅki vayatti laṭakkiṟ
 paḷiṅkottuk kāyam paḻukkiṉum piñcām
civaciva veṉkilar tīviṉi yāḷar
 civaciva veṉṟiṭat tīviṉi māḷum
 civaciva veṉṟiṭat tēvaru māvar
 civaciva veṉṉac civakati tāṉē	(tiruman. 2716)
eṉṉum tirumantiraṅkaḷāṉ uṇarka. itaṉ viḷakkattit tiruvāḷar ci.e. cuntara mutaliyār eḻutiya tirumantira upatēcap pakutiyuriyiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka. (pak. 52-4).
civam allatu cēyōṉ (murukaṉ) eṉpatu paṇṭit tamiḻtteyvam eṉpatum, āriya vētaṅkaḷiluḷḷa ruttiraṉ eṉṉum teyvamum tamiḻar teyvamākiya civam eṉpatum vēṟu eṉpatum piṉṉark kāṭṭappaṭum.
civāya nama eṉṉum tiruvinteḻuttil civa eṉṉum īreḻuttukaḷē tamiḻarkkuriyaṉa. āya eṉṉum nāṉkām vēṟṟumiyurupum, nama eṉṉum vaṇakkaṅkuṟitta collum, vaṭamoḻiyākum. tamiḻarkkut tiruvinteḻut tiruntiruppiṉ, atu civapōṟṟi eṉpatu pōṉṟirukkumēyaṉṟic civāya nama veṉṟirukkavē yirukkātu. āriyar tamiḻa matattalivarāṉa piṟkālattil, vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻi yeṉappaṭṭu, tamiḻiṟ pukunta ovvoru vaṭacollum vaṭaveḻuttum teyvattaṉmi yēṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭatu. carittiramum moḻinūlumaṟiyāta tamiḻarkku iṉṟum nāṉ colvatu viḷaṅkāteṉṟē niṉik kiṉṟēṉ. āṅkilavaṟiñarum Nœ¢áa¥òytU(Engineer)khd māṇikka nāyakarē mayaṅkiṉa reṉṟāl piṟari yeṉcolvatu? mūccukaḷ iraṇṭēyātalum, civa eṉṉum col īreḻuttuc collēyātalum ataṉāl vaḷinili añceḻutteṇṇa lākāmiyum aṟintukoḷka.
ellā veḻuttum eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil, uṟaḻcci vārat takatteḻu vaḷiyici ariltapa nāṭi aḷapiṟ kōṭal eṉpatic `civa eṉṉum tiruviraṇṭeḻuttup pōṉṟa volikaḷit tavattōr olittalākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉka.
tolkāppiyap pāyirattil, nāṉmaṟi muṟṟiya ataṅkōṭ ṭācāṟku eṉṉum pakutiyil, nāṉmaṟiyeṉṟatu tamiḻ maṟiyiyē.
nāṉmaṟiyeṉpatu, aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉṉum nāṟporuḷum payakkum maṟiyi. ēlātiyeṉṉum kīḻkkaṇakku nūṟ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuc ceyyuḷiluḷḷa nāṉmaṟi eṉṉun toṭarkkum iṅṅaṉamē poruḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. maṟṟappaṭi, camaṇarkku nāṉku maṟikaḷiṉmi yaṟika. patiṉāṉku curamuḷḷa kōviyip patiṉāṟkōvi yeṉpatuṅ kāṇka.
āriyarkkut tolkāppiyar kālattil orē maṟitāṉirun tatu. ippōtum uṇmiyāṉapaṭi āriya maṟikaḷ rukku, atarvaṇam eṉa iraṇṭēyākum. yacur, cāmam eṉpaṉa perumpālum rukvēta mantiraṅkaḷik koṇṭaviyē. ruk vētattiluḷḷa mūlamantiraṅkaḷ 102818 eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. rukvēta mantiraṅkaḷuḷ vēḷvikkuriyavaṟṟip pirittu yajur vētameṉṟum, pāṭuvataṟkēṟṟavaṟṟip pirittu icivakuttuc cāmavēta meṉṟum peyariṭṭaṉar. atarvaṇa vētam perumpālum māntirīka muṟiyāṉa mantiraṅkaḷik koṇṭatu; atu piṟkālattatu. tirāviṭar āriyar varumuṉṉamē māntirīkattil tērntiruntatiṉālum, āriyar mutalāvatu tirāviṭa māntirīkattip paḻittami rukvēta mantiraṅkaḷā laṟiyak kiṭappatālum atarvaṇam maṟṟa mūṉṟu vētaṅkaṭkum piṟpaṭṭatiṉālum, āriyar tirāviṭariṭamiruntu māntirīkattik   kaṟṟukkoṇṭa piṉpē atarvaṇa vētam tōṉṟiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. itaṉ mantiraṅkaḷilum ēṟattāḻa āṟiloru pākam rukvētattiluḷḷavi eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu.
āriya vētaṅkaḷi nāṉkāka vakuttatu viyācar eṉṟum, ataṉāl avar vētaviyācar eṉappaṭṭār eṉṟum collap paṭuvatiṉāl, tolkāppiyar kālattil āriya vētam oṉṟākavē yiruntatu eṉpatu tēṟṟam. ākiyāl, tol kāppiyap pāyirattiluḷḷa `nāṉmaṟi eṉpatu nāṉkāṉa āriya vētaṅkaḷik kuṟikkamuṭiyātu.

paṭṭāṅkunūl (Philosophy)
tamiḻ ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷiliruntē, paṇṭit tamiḻarkkup paṭṭāṅkunūl terintami yuṇarappaṭum.
poruḷkaḷellām  uyir,  mey (body),  uyirmey (pirāṇi) eṉa mūvakippaṭum. ivi, muṟiyē, taṉiyuyirum taṉiyuṭampum uyirōṭu kūṭiya uṭampumākum. pirāṇaṉi yuṭiyatu pirāṇi yeṉappaṭṭāṟpōla, uyiriyuṭiya mey          uyirmey eṉappaṭṭatu. (itu 2ām vēṟṟumi urupum payaṉum uṭaṉtokka toki.) pirāṇi eṉṉum vaṭacol vaḻaṅkavē, uyirmey eṉṉum col vaḻakkaṟṟut taṉ poruḷiyum       iḻantu, ilakkaṇanūl vaḻakkilum, uyirum meyyum cērnta    eḻuttu eṉṟu ummittokippuṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻit tokiyākat tavaṟākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ic collukkup patilāka uyir eṉṉum collum, uyirppirāṇi eṉṉuñ collum, muṟiyē, kuṉṟak kūṟalākavum mikipaṭak kūṟalākavum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
eḻuttukaḷil, uyirum meyyum uyirmeyyum pōlvaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷālēyē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. ip peyarkaḷ eḻuttik kuṟikkumpōtu uvamiyāku peyar. eḻuttiṟku uyirāvatu oli.
poyyāṉa uṭampi meyyeṉṟatu meypporuḷ nūlukku muraṇeṉṟu kūṟuvar cilar. uṭampu poyyāṉa teṉṟu muṉṉit tamiḻar aṟintiruntālum, uṭampōṭu kūṭiya niliyē uyirvāḻkkiyātaliṉ, uṭampi meyyeṉṟu maṅkala vaḻakkāyk kūṟiṉar eṉka.  cāvip perumpiṟitu eṉṟum cuṭukāṭṭi naṉkāṭu eṉṟum avar kūṟiyatuṅ kāṇka.
ōr āḷil, allatu uyirpporuḷil, uyir uṭampu eṉa iruporuḷ kalantiruppiṉum, kāṇpārkku uṭampē muṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟum; ataṉ piṉṉarē ataṟkuḷ uyiriruppatu aṟiyap paṭum. atupōla, uyirmeyyeḻuttilum meyyeḻuttē muṉ olikkum; ataṉ piṉṉarē uyir olikkum.  iti,
meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu uyirtōṉṟu niliyē  (tol. nūṉ. 18)
eṉṟaṉar ilakkaṇikaḷ .
ellā moḻikaḷilum meyyum uyirumāy varum eḻuttukkaḷ iṇintē olikkum. iti mēṉāṭṭār nōkkātatiṉāl, uyirmeykkut taṉivaṭivam vakukkāmal, meyyiyum uyiriyum aṭuttaṭuttu vevvēṟā yeḻutuvā rāyiṉar. itu māṇavarkku eḷitāyiṉum, āṅkila tamiḻa ilakkaṇikaḷiṉ meypporuḷuṇarccit tāḻvēṟṟaṅkaḷik kuṟikkat tavaṟāteṉka.
coṟkaḷukku ilakkaṇattil peyar viṉi eṉṟu makkaḷ toṭarpāṉa peyarkaḷē iṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. peyar = āḷ. peyar = pēr.  ettaṉi pēr eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. viṉi = ceyki.
poruḷkaḷip pakuttaṟivuṇmi yiṉmipaṟṟi, uyar   tiṇi, aḵṟiṇi eṉa iraṇṭāka vakuttaṉar tamiḻar. ip  pākupāṭu vēṟu moḻiyil illi. coṟkaḷiyum avi kuṟikkum poruḷkaḷpaṟṟi iru tiṇiyāka vakuttaṉar.
tamiḻil, coṟkaḷ avi kuṟikkum poruḷkaḷpaṟṟiyē pāluṇarttum, āriya moḻikaḷilō, avi īṟupaṟṟiyē poruḷuṇarttum.
vaṭamoḻiyil maṉiviyik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷum,  tāram, pāriyi, kaḷattiram eṉṉum mūṉṟum muṟiyē āṇpālum peṇpālum alippālumām.
uyirkaḷi ōraṟivuyir mutalāka āṟaṟivuyi rīṟāka aṟuvakiyākap pakuttaṉar tamiḻar.

oḻukka nūl (Ethics)
tolkāppiyattil,  puṟattiṇiyiyalil      44ām nūṟpāvil mūṉṟaṉ pakuti eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatāl, oḻukkanūl akkālattil tamiḻiliruntami uṇarappaṭum. mūṉṟaṉ       pakutiyāvatu aṟattiṉāl poruḷākki ap poruḷāl iṉpam nukartal eṉṟal. aṟamporu ḷiṉpam eṉpatu oḻukkanūṟ pākupāṭu. aṟam eṉavē, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam iraṇṭum aṭaṅkum. tuṟavaṟam - vīṭṭuneṟi.
aṟamporu ḷiṉpam vīṭeṉṉum pākupāṭu tamiḻaratē. āriyarē avaṟṟit tarmam, arttam, kāmam, mōkṣam eṉa vaṭamoḻiyil moḻipeyarttukkoṇṭaṉar. atōṭu ippōtu vaṭacoṟkaḷi mūlamākavum teṉcoṟkaḷi moḻi peyarppākavuṅ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻp poruṭpākupāṭu kaḷi yellām iṅṅaṉam moḻipeyarttuk koṇṭu, mūlamākak kāṭṭuvatu āriya vaḻakkam eṉka.
tamiḻar aṟattiṟ ciṟantiruntami, tolkāppiyattil āṅkāṅkuk kūṟappaṭum aṟavoḻukkaṅkaḷā laṟiyappaṭum.
poruḷkaḷi akam puṟam eṉṟu pakuppatē tamiḻa muṟiyeṉṟum, aṟamporu ḷiṉpam vīṭeṉṟu pakuppatu āriya muṟiyeṉṟum cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. oru moḻiyilēyē nūlukkum kalikkum ēṟṟapaṭi, vevvēṟu vakiyāyp poruḷkaḷ pakukkappaṭum. ilakkaṇanūl oṉṟilēyē, eḻuttatikārattil uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṟu mūṉṟu vakiyākavum, collatikārattil poruḷ iṭaṅ kālam ciṉi kuṇam toḻil eṉṟu āṟu vakiyākavum, maṟṟōr muṟiyil uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉṟu iruvakiyākavum poruḷkaḷ pakukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tarukkanūlil ēḻākavum patiṉāṟākavum pakukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākiyāl, akam puṟam eṉpatu poruḷati kārap pākupāṭeṉṟum, aṟamporu ḷiṉpam vīṭeṉpatu oḻukkanūṟ pākupāṭeṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
coṟkaḷ ilakkaṇa muṟiyil, peyar viṉi yiṭi yuri yeṉṟum, colliyal muṟiyil, iyal tiri tici vaṭa coṟkaḷeṉṟum, oḻukkanūliṟ poy kuṟaḷi kaṭuñcol payaṉilcol leṉṟum, koṇmuṭipu (cittānta) nūlil vēṟuvakiyākavum pakukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉamē poruḷkaḷu meṉka.
āriya nūlkaḷi muṟpaṭiyāka vittukkoṇṭu tamiḻ nūlkaḷukkup poruḷurippavarellām, evvakiyēṉum tavaṟavē ceyvar. tirukkuṟaḷ taṉittamiḻ muṟiyil iyaṟṟap paṭṭirukkavum, parimēlaḻakar ati āriyavaḻiyākak koṇṭu, aṟattuppāl mukavuriyilum kāmattuppāl mukavuriyilum uṇmikku māṟāṉavaṟṟi urittuḷḷār, aintiṇiyum taṉittaṉi kūṟappaṭumpōtu kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, pāliyeṉṟu peyar peṟumeṉṟum, kōviyāyk kūṟappaṭum pōtu kaḷavu, kaṟpu eṉa iru kikōḷkaḷākak kūṟappaṭu meṉṟumavaraṟiyātupōyiṉar.nfhitÆY«, muṉṉiya nāṉkum muṉṉataṟ keṉpa eṉpatāl kikkiḷi muṉṉum, kūṭṭattiṉāl aintiṇi iṭiyum, kūṭṭam nīṭikkātu maṭalēṟṟam nikaḻiṉ peruntiṇi piṉṉum amintiruttalum, muṟiyē nikaḻkiṉṟa puṇartal pirital iruttal allatu iraṅkal, ūṭal eṉpavi muṟiyē kuṟiñci, pāli, mulli, neytal, marutam eṉṉum aintiṇikaḷā yamitalum avar aṟintilar pōlum.

kaṇitanūl (Arithmetic)
kaṇ + itam = kaṇitam. kaṇ = karutu, mati, aḷa. kaṇ + iyam = kaṇṇiyam (matippu). kaṇ - kaṇi, to calculate. kaṇi + ki = kaṇiki (tāḷaṅkaṇittāṭum kūtti). kaṇi + aṉ = kaṇiyaṉ (jōtiṭaṉ). kaṇ + akku = kaṇakku. akku eṉpatu ōr īṟu. kā: ilakku ili, patakku. kaṇi - kuṇi - aḷaviṭu. kaṇ, kaṇi eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷiyē gan, gani eṉa urappi yolittu vaṭacollākak kāṭṭuvar. kaṇṇutal eṉṉuñ col, akakkaṇṇiṉ toḻilik kuṟittalāl vaṭamoḻi vaṭivaṅkaḷ teṉcoṟkaḷiṉ tiripē eṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum. kaṇpaṭi kaṇṇiya, kapili kaṇṇiya eṉṟu tol kāppiyattilēyē varutal kāṇka.
ari, kuṟi, kalam, paṉi, kā, patakku, tūṇi, uri, nāḻi eṉṉum aḷavuppeyarkaḷum niṟippeyarkaḷum, tol kāppiyattil, tokimarapu, uyirmayaṅkiyal eṉṉum iyalkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

kōḷkaḷ ēḻeṉṟē tamiḻarkoṇṭu, avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷāl ēḻunāṭkoṇṭa vārameṉṉum aḷavi ēṟpaṭuttiṉar. āṉāl, āriyar kōḷkaḷiṉ tokiyi mikuttuk kāṭṭavēṇṭumeṉṟu, katiravaṉ maṟivum tiṅkaḷ maṟivu mākiyavaṟṟit tam tiripuṇarcciyāl irukōḷkaḷākak karuti avaṟṟukku rāku kētu eṉṟu peyariṭṭu, avaṟṟi eḻukōḷkaḷōṭu kūṭṭi navakkirakameṉṟaṉar.
putaṉ caṉi eṉṉum iru peyarkaṭkum, paṇṭikkālattil vaḻaṅkivanta tamiḻp peyarkaḷ aṟivaṉ kāri eṉpaṉa.
paṉṉiru mātaṅkaṭkum ippōtu vaḻaṅkivarum vaṭamoḻip peyarkaṭkup patilāka, paṉṉīr ōri(rāci)yiṉ peyarkaḷē maliyāḷa nāṭṭiṟpōlap paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭilum vaḻaṅkivantaṉa. paṉṉīr ōrippeyarkaḷil mēṭam, iṭapam, kaṭakam, kaṉṉi, tulām, kumpam, mīṉam eṉṉum ēḻum tamiḻc coṟkaḷē. ivaṟṟuḷ mutalāṟukku muṟiyē, takar, kuṇṭi, alavaṉ, maṭanti, tūkku, kuṭam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. 
ēṉiya mituṉam, ciṅkam, viruccikam, taṉucu, makaram eṉṉum aintum tamiḻil, muṟiyē, iraṭṭi, āḷi, tēḷ, vil, cuṟā eṉappaṭum.

mātam eṉpatu mati eṉpatiṉiṉṟum piṟanta tamiḻc      collē. mati - mātam (ta.) - māsam (va.) mati = cantiraṉ. kālatti matippataṟkuk karuviyāyiruppatu mati. matittal aḷaviṭutal.       o.nō: moon-month. mati eṉpatiṉiṉṟu mātam eṉṉum vaṭivam tōṉṟu mēyaṉṟi māsam eṉṉum vaṭivan tōṉṟātu. ta-sa, pōli. tiṅkaḷ eṉṉum col oru kiḻamip peyarātaliṉ,       mātam eṉṉum collē mātattik kuṟippatākum. oru  vaḷarpiṟiyum oru tēypiṟiyum cērntatu oru mātam. matiyāluṇṭākum kāla aḷavu mātameṉappaṭṭatu. mati eṉṉum peyarum mātattik kuṟikkum. vaḷarpiṟikkālatti oḷippakka meṉṟum, tēypiṟik kālatti iruṭpakkameṉṟum22 muṉṉōr kūṟiṉar. ivaṟṟiyē, muṟiyē, cukkilapakṣa meṉṟum kiruṣṇapakṣa meṉṟam, nilimoḻikaḷi moḻi peyarttum varumoḻikaḷi eḻuttup peyarttuṅ koṇṭaṉar āriyar.
cila ōrippeyarkaḷiṉ mūlaṅkaḷāvaṉa:
mēḻakam - mēṭakam - mēṭam - mēṣam (va.) o.nō: 
puḻali - puṭali, nāḻuri - nāṭuri, viṭi - viṭapam (vruṣapam, va.) - iṭapam,  kum - kumpu - kumpam. kumpu + am = kumpam. kumputal - kuvital. miṉ - mīṉ - mīṉam. o.nō: tiṉ - tīṉ.
kaṭakam (naṇṭu) eṉpatu vaṭṭamāṉatu eṉṉum poruḷatu. tōṭkaṭakam, kaṭakappeṭṭi eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka. nāḻiki vaṭṭilik kuṟikkum kaṭiki eṉṉuñ collum kaṭakam eṉpataṉ maṟuvaṭivamē. vaṭṭamāṉatu vaṭṭil. kaṭiki + āram = kaṭikiyāram - kaṭikāram. tōṭkaṭakam tōḷvaḷivi eṉṟum kaṭiki eṉṟum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. kaṭakam oru parumip peyarumākum (Augmentative).
tuli eṉṉum coṟkup pakuti, tōl eṉṟu intiyil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ perumpakutiyik kaṭal koṇṭatiṉālum, paṇṭit tamiḻnūlkaḷil tolkāppiyan tavira maṟṟaviyellām aḻintupōṉatiṉālum, tamiḻc coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟku vērkaḷ tamiḻil maṟintuviṭṭaṉa. vaṭanāṭṭil, āriyar varumuṉ tirāviṭamoḻikaḷē vaḻaṅki vantamiyāl cinti, inti, vaṅkam mutaliya vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷil pala tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ kalantuḷḷaṉa. avi in nūliṉ mūṉṟām maṭalattil virivāyk kūṟappaṭum.

iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil, kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉ paṉi eṉṉum aṟuvakip paruvaṅkaḷiyum, muṟiyē, vacantam kirīṣmam, varṣam, carat, hēmantam, ciciram eṉṟu moḻi peyarttuk koṇṭaṉar āriyar. vacantam (Spring), kōṭi   (Summer), vaṟaḷi (Autumn), māri (Winter) eṉṉum nālvakip paruvaṅkaḷtām āriyanāṭu eṉṉum vaṭanāṭṭiṟkēṟkum. iḷavēṉil mutaliya aṟuvakip paruvaṅkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkē yuriyaṉa. itaṉāl, mūlam eviyeṉṟum moḻipeyarppu eviyeṉṟum aṟintukoḷḷalām. iṅṅaṉamē āriya moḻi peyarppukaḷ cilaviṭaṅkaḷil maṟintiruppiṉum cilaviṭaṅkaḷil veḷiyāyviṭumeṉka.

eṉṟum, aṟuvakit tiripukaḷum (ec. 7), mūvakik kuṟikaḷum (ecca. 57) viṉi payaṉ mey uru eṉṟa nālvaki yuvamaṅkaṭku vevvē ṟurupukaḷum (uva. 12-6) kūṟiyiruppatālum, piṟavaṟṟālum, paṇṭit tamiḻarkkuc colliyaluṇarcci yiruntami yaṟiyappaṭum. moḻinūṟku muṟpaṭiyāṉatu colliyalē. oru moḻipaṟṟiyatu colliyalum pala moḻipaṟṟiyatu moḻinūlumāku meṉṟaṟika.
tarukkanūl (Logic)
tarukkutal oruvaṉ taṉṉi mikuttukkāṭṭutal. taṉṉoṭum avaḷoṭum tarukkiya puṇarttu eṉṟu tolkāppiyattilēyē (akat. 53) vantiruttal kāṇka. tarukku - cerukku. ta-ca, a-e, pōlikaḷ. (o.nō: ōti - ōci, cattāṉ - cettāṉ.)
tarukku + am = tarukkam. tarukkattiṟku ēraṇam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. ēr + aṇam = ēraṇam. ēraṇam eḻucci. tarukkam eṉṉum teṉcolliyē, tarkkam eṉṟu eḻuti vaṭacol lākak kāṭṭuvar āriyar.
tarukkam eṉpatu oru coṟpōr. oruvaṉ taṉ koḷ kiyi nāṭṭi etiriyi velvatē tarukkam. velvatellām vāki. vākip potuvilakkaṇamāvatu,
tāvil koḷkit tattam kūṟṟip 
 pākupaṭa mikutip paṭuttal.	(tol. puṟat. 15)
akattiyat tarukka nūṟpākkaḷ
kīḻvarum nūṟpākkaḷ tarukkap pulavar vaṭivēl ceṭṭiyār patippitta `tarkka paripāṣi eṉṉum nūliṟutiyiṟ kaṇṭavi. ivi uṇmiyil akattiyar iyaṟṟiyaviyāyiṉ, tolkāppiyar kālattil tarukkanūlun tamiḻiliruntami yaṟiyappaṭum.
  1.	poruḷkuṇaṅ karumam potucciṟap poṟṟumi
	yiṉmi yuṭaṉporu ḷēḻeṉa moḻipa.
  2.	maṇpuṉa laṉalkāl veḷipoḻu tāci
	yāṉmā maṉatō ṭoṉpatum poruḷē.
  3.	vaṭivañ cuviyiru nāṟṟa mūṟeṇ
	ṇaḷavu vēṟṟumi puṇarcci pirivu
	muṉmi piṉmi tiṇmi nekiḻcci
	cikkeṉa lōci yuṇarcci yiṉpan
	tuṉpam viruppam veṟuppu muyaṟci
	yaṟamaṟam vātaṉiyoṭu kuṇamaṟu nāṉkē.
  4.	eḻumpal viḻutal vaḷita ṉimirta
	ṉaṭatta luṭaṉē karumami vakittē.
  5.	potumi mēlkī ḻeṉaviru vakiyē.
  6.	maṉṉiya poruḷiṉ kaṇṇavā yavaṟṟiṉ
	vēṟṟumi terippaṉa palavāñ ciṟappē.
  7.	oṟṟumi yāppaḵ toṉṟē yeṉka.
  8.	muṉṉiṉmi piṉṉiṉmi muṟṟu miṉmi
	yoṉṟiṉoṉ ṟiṉmiyeṉ ṟiṉmi nāṉkē
   9.	maṇṇī raṉalkāṉ muṟiyē nāṟṟan
	taṭpam veppa mūṟṟa māki
	meypporu ḷaḻiporuṇ mēvu meṉka.
 10.	aṇukkaṇ meypporuḷ kāriya maḻiporuḷ
	pirutivi nittiya vanittiya vaṇampeṟum
	niliyaṇup poruṇili yillatu kāriyam.
 11.	atuvē: 
	uṭalpoṟi viṭaya mūvakip paṭumē
	nammaṉōr yākki maṇkūṟ ṟuṭampu
	nāṟṟaṅ kavarvatu nāciyi ṉuṉiyē
	maṇkal mutaliya viṭaya mākum.
 12.	nīriṟi varippiṟ kaṭṭunī ruṭampu
	cuvitiṟaṅ kavarvatu nāvi ṉuṉiyē
	kaṭalyā ṟāti viṭaya mākum.
 13.	katirōṉ varippiṟ kaṭṭaṉa luṭampē
	yuruvaṅ kavarvatu karumaṇi viḻiyē
	maṇviṇ vayiṟā karanāl viṭayam.
14.	vaḷiyiṟi varippiṟ kaṭṭukā luṭampē
	yūṟṟaṅ kavarvatu mīntō leṉka
	viṭaya maramuta lacitaṟ kētuvē
	pirāṇa ṉuṭalakat tiyaṅkuṅ kāṟṟē.
15.	vicumpē kālan ticiyō ṭāṉmā
	maṉamivi yintu nittiyap poruḷē.
16.	ōcip paṇpiṟ ṟākā yammē.
17.	iṟappumutal vaḻakkiṟ kētuk kālam.
18.	kiḻakkumutal vaḻakkiṟ kētut ticiyē.
19.	aṟivup paṇpiṟ ṟāṉmā veṉka
	viṟiyē yīcaṉmuṟ ṟaṟivaṉōr mutalē
	yuyirtā ṉuṭaṟoṟum vevvē ṟākum.
20.	maṉamaṇu vaṭivāy varumiṉ pāti
	yaṟitaṟ kiṉṟi yamiyāk karuvi
	yākip palavā yaḻiviṉ ṟuṟumē.
in nūṟpākkaḷiyē, nuṇṇaṟivuḷḷa oruvar oru nūlāka virittuviṭalām.
in nūṟpākkaḷ ciṟappika muṟiyic cērntavi. ciṟap pikam (vicēṭikam), muṟiyikam (niyāyam) eṉṉum iruvakit tarukka muṟikaḷuḷ, muṉṉatē ciṟantateṉka.
toḻiṟkalikaḷ (Arts)
ciṟpam (Architecture)
muḻumutal araṇam (puṟat. 8), āreyil (puṟat. 10), matiṟkuṭumi (puṟat. 10) mutaliya kuṟippukaḷāl, tolkāppiyar kālattil ciṟpattoḻil tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ ciṟantiruntami yuṇarappaṭum.
cuvar, matil, pataṇam, iñci, akappā, purici, eyil, cō mutaliya matilvakikaḷum, ñāyil, cūṭṭu, ēppuḻi mutaliya matiluṟuppukaḷum, eḻunili māṭamum paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ ciṟpakkalic ciṟappi uṇarttuvaṉavākum. ivaṟṟiṉ virivic cilappatikārattuṭ kaṇṭukoḷka.
paṇṭit tamiḻar nāṭakavaraṅku amitta nuṭpam (cilap. 113-3:6) mika viyakkattakkatu.
vaḷiviṟpoṟi, karuviralūkam, kallumiḻkavaṇ, pākaṭu kuḻici, kāypoṉṉuli, kalliṭukūṭi, tūṇṭil, toṭakku, āṇṭaliyaṭuppu, kavi, kaḻu, aiyavittulām, kipeyarūci, ceṉṟeṟiciral, paṉṟi, paṉi, nūṟṟuvarikkolli, taḷḷi veṭṭi, kaḷiṟṟuppoṟi, viḻuṅkumpāmpu, kaḻukupoṟi, pulippoṟi, kuṭappāmpu, cakaṭappoṟi, takarppoṟi, arinūṟpoṟi24 mutaliya matiṟpoṟi vakikaḷ, paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ goik¡fiyiaí«(Sculpture) cūḻcciyak kaliyiyum (Engineering) terivippaṉavākum. paṭimi - patumi. pāviviḷakku kantiṟpāvi mutaliya peyarkaḷiyum nōkkuka.
ciṟpam paṭimikkali mutaliyavaṟṟālum, nāṭakavaraṅkiṉ eḻiṉiyālum XÉaüY«(Drawing) paṇṭit tamiḻarkkut terintiruntami collāmē yuṇarappaṭum. ciṟpanūl ōviya nūl eṉṟu irunūlkaḷ kaṭikkaḻakak kālattiliruntaṉavāka, aṭiyārkku nallār araṅkēṟṟu kātiyuriyiṟ kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟār.
uḻavu (Agriculture)
 uḻavu paṇṭit tamiḻarkkut terintiruntamiyi oruvarum aiyuṟār. āyiṉum, atippaṟṟiya cila kuṟippukaḷ aṟiyat takkaṉa.
ērōr kaḷavaḻi eṉpatu tolkāppiyam(puṟat. 16). 
Agriculture eṉṉum āṅkilappeyar tamiḻ mūlattatākavē terikiṉṟatu.
Agriculture, L. agricultura - ager, a field, cultura, cultivation - colo, to cultivate.
24	cilap. pak. 413 - 415.
akaram marutanilattūr. marutanilam vayaṟpāṅku. pārppaṉar marutanilattil taṅkiṉatiṉālēyē, avar kuṭiyiruppu ak kirakāram eṉappaṭṭatu. akkira + akaram = akkirākaram. 
akkiram = nuṉi, mutal, talimi.
akara māyiram antaṇark kīyileṉ
eṉṟār tirumūlar.
E. acre, A. S. aceer, Ger. acker, Gk. agros, L. ager, Skt. ajra.
kal = tōṇṭu. kal + vi = kalvi. kalvi eṉṉum peyar mutalāvatu tōṇṭutal eṉṉum poruḷil uḻavik kuṟittatu. piṉpu, nūṟkalvikkuc ciṟappup peyarāyiṟṟu. māntaṉ mutaṉmutaṟ kaṟṟa kalvi uḻavē. kal - colo, to till.
toḻil eṉṉum peyarum mutalāvatu uḻaviyē kuṟittatu. toḷ + iḷal = toḷil - toḻil.
o.nō: poḷ + il = poḷil - poḻil. poḷ - poḷi. poḷittal veṭṭutal. (poḷ - poḻ) poḷḷa (veṭṭa)ppaṭuvatu poḻil. poḻil = cōli. paṇṭit tamiḻakam muḻuvatum peruñcōliyāy allatu kāṭāyiruntatu. kāṭuveṭṭi nāṭākkappaṭṭatu. akattiyar kāṭukeṭuttu nāṭākkip potiyiṉkaṇiruntami nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟutal kāṇka.
tamiḻakam muḻutum oru peruñcōliyā yiruntatiṉā lēyē poḻil eṉṉum peyar ulakam allatu nāṭu eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuvatāyiṟṟu. nāvalan taṇpoḻil vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇ poḻil, ēḻpoḻil eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
toḷ = tōṇṭu. toḷ - toṭu = tōṇṭu. toḷ- till, to cultivate. toḷḷuvatu toḻil.
māntaṉ mutalāvatu ceyta peruntoḻil uḻavē. uḻaviṉ peyarāṉa toḻil eṉpatu, piṟkālattil ellāt toḻilkaṭkum potuppeyarāyiṟṟu.
cey = vayal. ceyvatu cey. naṉcey, puṉcey eṉṉum peyarkaḷi nōkkuka. ceyki = vayalvēli. paṇ = cey. paṇṇuvatu paṇṇi. paṇṇi = vayal.
mutalāvatu kiyiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa toḻil uḻavu eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṟṟiyē, uḻavarik kiceytūṇ māliyavar eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
mutalāvatu tōṉṟiya oru poruḷiṉ allatu toḻiliṉ peyar, piṟkālattil ataṉ iṉattiṟkup potuppeyarāy vaḻaṅkutal iyalpē. tōṭu eṉṉum ōlip peyar, piṟkālattil ataṟkup patilāyaṇippaṭum kammal (kamalam) vakikkup potup peyarāy vaḻaṅkuvatiyum; villēruḻavar, collēruḻavar, tērōr kaḷavaḻi yeṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷiyum nōkkuka.
paḻa nārvījiyam (Old Norse), āṅkilō-cāksaṉ eṉṟa moḻikaḷil, muṟiyē, erfidhi, earfodh eṉṟa coṟkaḷ mutalāvatu uḻavik kuṟittup piṉpu uḻippik kuṟittaṉa veṉṟum. lattīṉil ars, artis eṉṟa coṟkaḷ mutalāvatu uḻavik kuṟittup piṉpu kalip potuppeya rāyiṟṟeṉṟum, ac coṟkaṭku mūlam ar(to plough) eṉpatu eṉṟum, mutalāvatu kaṟpikkappaṭṭa kali uḻavēyeṉṟum mākkacu mullar kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.25
ēr = kalappi. ērttoḻil = uḻavu.

E. share - bfhG. M.E. schar, A.S. scear, Ger. schar, schaar.
kāṟu = koḻu.
ka-ca, pōli. o.nō: tiki - tici (tēvā. 308:1), ikal - ical. cēmpars akarātiyil shear (to cut) eṉpatu share eṉpataṉ mūlamākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, shear  eṉpatu ciṟi eṉṉum tamiḻc colli ottiruttal kāṇka.

necavu (Weaving)
uṭipeyart tuṭuttal eṉpatu tolkāppiyam 
(mey. 14). parutti necavu mutaṉmutal intiyāviltāṉ ceyyappaṭṭa teṉṟum, aṅkiruntē mēṉāṭukaḷukkup paraviya teṉṟum vayavar cāṇ mārcal kūṟukiṟār.
paṇṭikkālac carittirāciriyarellām. mēṉāṭukaḷ intiyāviṉiṉṟum iṟakkumati ceyta poruḷkaḷuḷ, tuṇi oṉṟākat tavaṟātu kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar.
18ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutivari, intiyāviṉiṉṟē mēṉāṭukaṭkut tuṇi ēṟṟumatiyāṉami, piṉvaruṅ kuṟippiṉā laṟiyalām.
taṟkāla intiyāviṉ vaṟumikku maṟṟoru peruṅ kāraṇam, airōppāvil poṟiviṉit tōṟṟattiṉālum, tiruntiya muṟic ceyporuḷ viṉiyiṉ paravaliṉālum ēṟpaṭṭa kittoḻil tēvikkuṟivē. airōppā, intiyariṉ contat tuṟikaḷil, iṉṉum, eṇṇukkeṭṭāta toṉṟu toṭṭu evaṟṟukku avarkaḷic cārntiruntōmō aviyum, avarkaḷukkē vitappā yuriyavumāṉa kittoḻilkaḷilum ceyporuḷ viṉikaḷilum, avarkaḷi vellak kaṟṟuk koṇṭatiṉāl, uṇmiyil iṉi yorupōtum etaṟkum intiyāvic cāntiruppatilli. uṇmiyil, kāriyaṅkaḷ talikīḻāy māṟiviṭṭaṉa. iv veḷippāṭu intiyāvi muṟṟilum keṭuttuviṭuvatāka accuṟuttukiṉṟatu.
airōppāviṟkut tirumpuvataṟkuc caṟṟu muṉpē, kittoḻil ceyyum kūṟṟaṅkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟiṉūṭu vaḻic ceṉṟēṉ. aṅkup paraviyirunta pāḻnilikku ētum oppākātu. vēliyaṟikaḷellām cāttappaṭṭiruntaṉa. aṅkē vāḻum ilakkakkaṇakkāṉa necavuttoḻiṉ makkaḷ paciyāl maṭintu koṇṭiruntaṉar. nāṭṭiluḷḷa muṉṟavaṟṟeṇṇam (prejudice) paṟṟi, avarkaḷ avamāṉappaṭṭaṉṟip piṟa toḻiliyum mēṟkoḷḷa muṭiyātu. muntiya kālattil nūlnūṟṟut taṅkaḷ kuṭumpattik kāttuvanta, kaṇakkaṟṟa kimpeṇkaḷum piṟa peṇṭirum vēliyiḻantu piḻippaṟṟiruntatik kaṇṭēṉ. yāṉ eṅkuc ceṉṟālum orē tuṉpattōṟṟam eṉakku etirē niṉṟatu26 eṉṟu 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil micūr nāṭṭil viṭiyūḻiyam ceyta appē ṭūpāys (Abbe Dubois) kūṟu kiṉṟār.
paṇṭik kālattiṟ peṇṭir nūl nūṟṟamiyi nakkīrar, kapilar, pavaṇanti mutaliyōruṅ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. paruttip peṇṭu eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟu (125, 326) kūṟum.

atilum tipput toḻiluḷḷamiyiṉ. vaṇṭiyit tikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu taccar kūṟum vaḻakki nōkkuka. tittal pala vuṟuppukaḷi oṉṟāyiṇittal. necavil nūlkaḷ oṉṟāyiṇikkappaṭalum, taccil vaṇṭiyāyiṉ kuṟaṭu āri cūṭṭu eṉṉum uṟuppukaḷum, peṭṭi nāṟkāliyāyiṉ palaki kaḷum kālkaḷum oṉṟāyiṇikkappaṭalum kāṇka.
vāṇikam (Commerce)
vāṇi eṉṉum col vāynīr eṉpatiṉiṉṟu piṟantatākavum koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu. vāynīri vāṇīr eṉpatu kocci vaḻakku. o.nō: kuṟunoy - kuṟuṇi. taṇṇīr taṇṇi eṉappaṭuvatupōla, vāṇīr vāṇi eṉappaṭum. vāynīrukku maṟupeyar coḷḷu eṉpatu. coḷucoḷu eṉṟu vaṭivatu coḷḷu. pēcat toṭaṅkum paruvattil kuḻantikaṭkuc coḷḷu vaṭiyum. coḷḷu mikutiyāy vaṭintāl pēccu mikutiyākum eṉṟoru koḷkiyuḷatu. coḷḷum peruttāl collup perukkum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. coḷḷu - collu -col. vāynīr - vāṇīr - vāṇi = nīr, col. vāṇi - pāṇi.
kocci vaḻakkiliruntum cila coṟkaḷ koḷḷappaṭum. kā. koṇṭuvā - (koṇṭā) - koṇā - koṇar, paḻam - payam.
vāṇi eṉpataṟku vāc eṉṟu vaṭamoḻi mūlaṅ kāṭṭiṉum, ataṟkum vāy eṉṉum tamiḻccollē mūlamātal kāṇka.
vāṇi + ikam = vāṇikam. vāṇi = col. collutal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum vāṇikam eṉṉum col, vili collutal eṉṉum poruḷil paṇṭamāṟṟik kuṟippatākum. o.nō: pakartal = collutal, viṟṟal. noṭuttal = collutal, viṟṟal. noṭi = kati. noṭi = viṟpaṉi.
vāṇi eṉṉum col vaḻakkilillāmiyiṉālēyē vaṭacollākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu, pāṇi eṉṉuñ col pōla. varttakam, viyāpāram, viciyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷē vaṭa coṟkaḷ. vāṉi (vāṉ + i) eṉṉuñ col vāṇiyeṉṟu tirintirukkalām. oliyi vāṉattiṉ paṇpāka muṉṉōr karutiṉar. vāṇikam eṉpatu vaṇikam eṉṟu kuṟukiyum vaḻaṅkum.
vāṇikam mutalāvatu uḷnāṭṭu vaṇikam ayalnāṭṭu vaṇikam eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ ayalnāṭṭu vaṇikamē ciṟantatu. atuvum nilavāṇikam nīrvāṇikam eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ nīrvāṇikamē ciṟantatu.
tamiḻar paṇṭikkālattil nīrvāṇikattiṟ ciṟantirun tamikku akaccāṉṟum puṟaccāṉṟum nirampavuḷa.
kiṟittuvukku 3000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭatāṉa ūr eṉṉum kaltēyanakar akaḻvil, intiyat tēkku marattuṇṭu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭateṉṟum, tēkkumaram intiyāvil vintiyamalikku vaṭakkē vaḷarvatē yilliyeṉṟum, masli ṉukkup pāpilōṉiya paḻampeyar cintuveṉṟum, atu intiyāviṉiṉṟum ēṟṟumatiyāṉatiṉāl ap peyar peṟṟa teṉṟum rākosiṉ kūṟukiṟār. 28
